    30	    
    31	    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)
    32	    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
    33	        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;
    34	    }
    35	}
    36	
    37	void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
    38	    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
    39	        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
    40	            path[idx2(i, j, numNodes)] = j;
    41	            path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = i;
    42	        }
    43	        path[idx2(j, j, numNodes)] = j;
    44	    }
    45	}
    46	
    47	namespace {
    48	
    49	constexpr unsigned int TILE = 32;
    50	constexpr unsigned int THREAD_ROWS = 16;
    51	constexpr unsigned int OUTPUT_TILE = 2 * TILE;
    52	
    53	void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
    54	    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
    55	        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error (%s): %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
    56	        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
    57	    }
    58	}
    59	
    60	struct DeviceBuffer {
    61	    unsigned int* data = nullptr;
    62	    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t bytes) {
    63	        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&data, bytes), "allocate device matrix");
    64	    }
    65	    ~DeviceBuffer() { cudaFree(data); }
    66	    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
    67	    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
    68	};
    69	
    70	// A rank is one plus the largest internal vertex on a path; a direct edge has
    71	// rank zero. Among equal distances retain the smallest rank. This reproduces
    72	// the original ascending-k, strict-improvement path matrix even though tiling
    73	// changes the order in which paths are discovered.
    74	__device__ __forceinline__ void relax(unsigned int& distance, unsigned int& rank,
    75	                                     unsigned int candidate, unsigned int leftRank,
    76	                                     unsigned int rightRank, unsigned int viaRank) {
    77	    if (candidate <= distance) {
    78	        const unsigned int candidateRank = max(max(leftRank, rightRank), viaRank);
    79	        rank = candidate < distance ? candidateRank : min(rank, candidateRank);
    80	        distance = candidate;
    81	    }
    82	}
    83	
    84	__global__ void initializeDeviceMatrices(unsigned int* dist, unsigned int* ranks,
    85	                                         size_t stride, size_t numNodes) {
    86	    const size_t count = stride * stride;
    87	    for (size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    88	         index < count; index += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x) {
    89	        const size_t row = index / stride;
    90	        const size_t col = index % stride;
    91	        if (row >= numNodes || col >= numNodes) {
    92	            dist[index] = row == col ? 0 : INF;
    93	        }
    94	        ranks[index] = 0;
    95	    }
    96	}
    97	
    98	__global__ void updatePivot(unsigned int* dist, unsigned int* ranks,
    99	                            size_t stride, unsigned int pivot) {
   100	    __shared__ unsigned int tile[TILE][TILE];
   101	    __shared__ unsigned int tileRank[TILE][TILE];
   102	    const unsigned int col = threadIdx.x;
   103	    const size_t base = static_cast<size_t>(pivot) * TILE;
   104	    #pragma unroll
   105	    for (unsigned int r = 0; r < TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
   106	        const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
   107	        const size_t index = (base + row) * stride + base + col;
   108	        tile[row][col] = dist[index];
   109	        tileRank[row][col] = ranks[index];
   110	    }
   111	    __syncthreads();
   112	
   113	    #pragma unroll 1
   114	    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {
   115	        #pragma unroll
   116	        for (unsigned int r = 0; r < TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
   117	            const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
   118	            // With a zero diagonal these elements cannot improve. Leaving
   119	            // them untouched also keeps the operands read by all threads
   120	            // stable until the barrier, without shared-memory data races.
   121	            if (row != k && col != k) {
   122	                unsigned int value = tile[row][col];
   123	                unsigned int rank = tileRank[row][col];
   124	                relax(value, rank, tile[row][k] + tile[k][col],
   125	                      tileRank[row][k], tileRank[k][col], base + k + 1);
   126	                tile[row][col] = value;
   127	                tileRank[row][col] = rank;
   128	            }
   129	        }
   130	        __syncthreads();
   131	    }
   132	    #pragma unroll
   133	    for (unsigned int r = 0; r < TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
   134	        const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
   135	        const size_t index = (base + row) * stride + base + col;
   136	        dist[index] = tile[row][col];
   137	        ranks[index] = tileRank[row][col];
   138	    }
   139	}
   140	
   141	// Row and column tiles use the completed pivot tile. Blocks operate on disjoint
   142	// tiles, and each k only reads an unchanged row/column of its evolving tile.
   143	__global__ void updatePanels(unsigned int* dist, unsigned int* ranks,
   144	                             size_t stride, unsigned int pivot) {
   145	    __shared__ unsigned int diagonal[TILE][TILE];
   146	    __shared__ unsigned int diagonalRank[TILE][TILE];
   147	    __shared__ unsigned int panel[TILE][TILE];
   148	    __shared__ unsigned int panelRank[TILE][TILE];
   149	    const bool columnPanel = blockIdx.y != 0;
   150	    const unsigned int other = blockIdx.x + (blockIdx.x >= pivot);
   151	    const size_t pivotBase = static_cast<size_t>(pivot) * TILE;
   152	    const size_t rowBase = columnPanel ? static_cast<size_t>(other) * TILE : pivotBase;
   153	    const size_t colBase = columnPanel ? pivotBase : static_cast<size_t>(other) * TILE;
   154	    const unsigned int col = threadIdx.x;
   155	
   156	    #pragma unroll
   157	    for (unsigned int r = 0; r < TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
   158	        const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
   159	        const size_t diagonalIndex = (pivotBase + row) * stride + pivotBase + col;
   160	        const size_t index = (rowBase + row) * stride + colBase + col;
   161	        diagonal[row][col] = dist[diagonalIndex];
   162	        diagonalRank[row][col] = ranks[diagonalIndex];
   163	        panel[row][col] = dist[index];
   164	        panelRank[row][col] = ranks[index];
   165	    }
   166	    __syncthreads();
   167	
   168	    #pragma unroll 1
   169	    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {
   170	        #pragma unroll
   171	        for (unsigned int r = 0; r < TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
   172	            const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
   173	            if (columnPanel ? col != k : row != k) {
   174	                unsigned int value = panel[row][col];
   175	                unsigned int rank = panelRank[row][col];
   176	                if (columnPanel) {
   177	                    relax(value, rank, panel[row][k] + diagonal[k][col],
   178	                          panelRank[row][k], diagonalRank[k][col], pivotBase + k + 1);
   179	                } else {
   180	                    relax(value, rank, diagonal[row][k] + panel[k][col],
   181	                          diagonalRank[row][k], panelRank[k][col], pivotBase + k + 1);
   182	                }
   183	                panel[row][col] = value;
   184	                panelRank[row][col] = rank;
   185	            }
   186	        }
   187	        __syncthreads();
   188	    }
   189	    #pragma unroll
   190	    for (unsigned int r = 0; r < TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
   191	        const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
   192	        const size_t index = (rowBase + row) * stride + colBase + col;
   193	        dist[index] = panel[row][col];
   194	        ranks[index] = panelRank[row][col];
   195	    }
   196	}
   197	
   198	__device__ __forceinline__ size_t skipPivot(unsigned int coordinate,
   199	                                           unsigned int pivot) {
   200	    return coordinate + static_cast<size_t>(coordinate / TILE >= pivot) * TILE;
   201	}
   202	
   203	// Each block updates four 32x32 tiles using two 64x32 panels. All eight results
   204	// owned by a thread stay in registers throughout the reduction over k.
   205	__global__ __launch_bounds__(TILE * THREAD_ROWS, 2)
   206	void updateRemaining(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
   207	                     unsigned int* __restrict__ ranks,
   208	                     size_t stride, unsigned int pivot) {
   209	    __shared__ unsigned int left[OUTPUT_TILE][TILE];
   210	    __shared__ unsigned int leftRank[OUTPUT_TILE][TILE];
   211	    __shared__ unsigned int right[TILE][OUTPUT_TILE];
   212	    __shared__ unsigned int rightRank[TILE][OUTPUT_TILE];
   213	    unsigned int values[OUTPUT_TILE / THREAD_ROWS][2];
   214	    unsigned int valueRanks[OUTPUT_TILE / THREAD_ROWS][2];
   215	    const unsigned int tx = threadIdx.x;
   216	    const unsigned int ty = threadIdx.y;
   217	    const unsigned int rowBase = blockIdx.y * OUTPUT_TILE;
   218	    const unsigned int colBase = blockIdx.x * OUTPUT_TILE;
   219	    const size_t pivotBase = static_cast<size_t>(pivot) * TILE;
   220	
   221	    #pragma unroll
   222	    for (unsigned int r = 0; r < OUTPUT_TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
   223	        const unsigned int localRow = ty + r * THREAD_ROWS;
   224	        const size_t row = skipPivot(rowBase + localRow, pivot);
   225	        const size_t index = row * stride + pivotBase + tx;
   226	        left[localRow][tx] = row < stride ? dist[index] : INF;
   227	        leftRank[localRow][tx] = row < stride ? ranks[index] : 0;
   228	        #pragma unroll
   229	        for (unsigned int c = 0; c < 2; ++c) {
   230	            const size_t col = skipPivot(colBase + tx + c * TILE, pivot);
   231	            const size_t resultIndex = row * stride + col;
   232	            values[r][c] = row < stride && col < stride ? dist[resultIndex] : INF;
   233	            valueRanks[r][c] = row < stride && col < stride ? ranks[resultIndex] : 0;
   234	        }
   235	    }
   236	    #pragma unroll
   237	    for (unsigned int r = 0; r < TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
   238	        const unsigned int localRow = ty + r * THREAD_ROWS;
   239	        #pragma unroll
   240	        for (unsigned int c = 0; c < 2; ++c) {
   241	            const unsigned int localCol = tx + c * TILE;
   242	            const size_t col = skipPivot(colBase + localCol, pivot);
   243	            const size_t index = (pivotBase + localRow) * stride + col;
   244	            right[localRow][localCol] = col < stride ? dist[index] : INF;
   245	            rightRank[localRow][localCol] = col < stride ? ranks[index] : 0;
   246	        }
   247	    }
   248	    __syncthreads();
   249	
   250	    #pragma unroll 1
   251	    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {
   252	        const unsigned int viaRank = pivotBase + k + 1;
   253	        unsigned int b[2];
   254	        unsigned int bRank[2];
   255	        #pragma unroll
   256	        for (unsigned int c = 0; c < 2; ++c) {
   257	            b[c] = right[k][tx + c * TILE];
   258	            bRank[c] = max(rightRank[k][tx + c * TILE], viaRank);
   259	        }
   260	        #pragma unroll
   261	        for (unsigned int r = 0; r < OUTPUT_TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
   262	            const unsigned int a = left[ty + r * THREAD_ROWS][k];
   263	            const unsigned int aRank = leftRank[ty + r * THREAD_ROWS][k];
   264	            #pragma unroll
   265	            for (unsigned int c = 0; c < 2; ++c) {
   266	                relax(values[r][c], valueRanks[r][c], a + b[c], aRank, bRank[c], 0);
   267	            }
   268	        }
   269	    }
   270	    #pragma unroll
   271	    for (unsigned int r = 0; r < OUTPUT_TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
   272	        const size_t row = skipPivot(rowBase + ty + r * THREAD_ROWS, pivot);
   273	        #pragma unroll
   274	        for (unsigned int c = 0; c < 2; ++c) {
   275	            const size_t col = skipPivot(colBase + tx + c * TILE, pivot);
   276	            if (row < stride && col < stride) {
   277	                const size_t index = row * stride + col;
   278	                dist[index] = values[r][c];
   279	                ranks[index] = valueRanks[r][c];
   280	            }
   281	        }
   282	    }
   283	}
   284	
   285	__global__ void restorePathMatrix(unsigned int* ranks, size_t stride) {
   286	    const size_t count = stride * stride;
   287	    for (size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
   288	         index < count; index += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x) {
   289	        const unsigned int rank = ranks[index];
   290	        ranks[index] = rank ? rank - 1 : static_cast<unsigned int>(index / stride);
   291	    }
   292	}
   293	
   294	} // namespace
   295	
   296	void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
   297	                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
   298	                   const size_t numNodes) {
   299	    if (numNodes == 0) return;
   300	    const size_t stride = (numNodes + TILE - 1) / TILE * TILE;
   301	    const unsigned int tiles = stride / TILE;
   302	    const size_t pitch = stride * sizeof(unsigned int);
   303	    const size_t hostPitch = numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int);
   304	    DeviceBuffer deviceDist(stride * pitch);
   305	    DeviceBuffer deviceRanks(stride * pitch);
   306	    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy2D(deviceDist.data, pitch, dist.data(), hostPitch,
   307	                           hostPitch, numNodes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "upload distances");
   308	
   309	    const unsigned int linearBlocks = static_cast<unsigned int>(
   310	        std::min((stride * stride + 255) / 256, static_cast<size_t>(65535)));
   311	    initializeDeviceMatrices<<<linearBlocks, 256>>>(deviceDist.data, deviceRanks.data,
   312	                                                   stride, numNodes);
   313	    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "initialize device matrices");
   314	    const dim3 threads(TILE, THREAD_ROWS);
   315	    const dim3 remainingGrid(tiles / 2, tiles / 2);
   316	    for (unsigned int pivot = 0; pivot < tiles; ++pivot) {
   317	        // Launches in the default stream provide the global dependencies
   318	        // between phases; no host synchronization is needed inside the loop.
   319	        updatePivot<<<1, threads>>>(deviceDist.data, deviceRanks.data, stride, pivot);
   320	        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "update pivot tile");
   321	        if (tiles > 1) {
   322	            updatePanels<<<dim3(tiles - 1, 2), threads>>>(
   323	                deviceDist.data, deviceRanks.data, stride, pivot);
   324	            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "update pivot row and column");
   325	            updateRemaining<<<remainingGrid, threads>>>(
   326	                deviceDist.data, deviceRanks.data, stride, pivot);
   327	            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "update remaining tiles");
   328	        }
   329	    }
   330	    restorePathMatrix<<<linearBlocks, 256>>>(deviceRanks.data, stride);
   331	    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "restore path matrix");
   332	    // Synchronous downloads include execution in the reported benchmark time.
   333	    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy2D(dist.data(), hostPitch, deviceDist.data, pitch,
   334	                           hostPitch, numNodes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "download distances");
   335	    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy2D(path.data(), hostPitch, deviceRanks.data, pitch,
   336	                           hostPitch, numNodes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "download paths");
   337	}
   338	
   339	bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
   340	    // Basic sanity checks
   341	    
   342	    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
   343	    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
   344	        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {
   345	            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\n", i, i);
   346	            return false;
   347	        }
   348	    }
   349	    
   350	    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]
   351	    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time
   352	    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {
   353	        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {
   354	            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
   355	                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
   356	                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
   357	                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
   358	                
   359	                // Check for overflow before addition
   360	                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {
   361	                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {
   362	                        printf("Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\n", 
   363	                               i, j, k);
   364	                        return false;
   365	                    }
   366	                }
   367	            }
   368	        }
   369	    }
   370	    
   371	    return true;
   372	}
   373

   375	    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
   376	    printf("Options:\n");
   377	    printf("  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\n");
   378	    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   379	    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   380	    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   381	}

   390	        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {

+namespace {
+
+constexpr unsigned int TILE = 32;
+constexpr unsigned int THREAD_ROWS = 16;
+constexpr unsigned int OUTPUT_TILE = 2 * TILE;
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error (%s): %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+struct DeviceBuffer {
+    unsigned int* data = nullptr;
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t bytes) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&data, bytes), "allocate device matrix");
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { cudaFree(data); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+};
+
+// A rank is one plus the largest internal vertex on a path; a direct edge has
+// rank zero. Among equal distances retain the smallest rank. This reproduces
+// the original ascending-k, strict-improvement path matrix even though tiling
+// changes the order in which paths are discovered.
+__device__ __forceinline__ void relax(unsigned int& distance, unsigned int& rank,
+                                     unsigned int candidate, unsigned int leftRank,
+                                     unsigned int rightRank, unsigned int viaRank) {
+    if (candidate <= distance) {
+        const unsigned int candidateRank = max(max(leftRank, rightRank), viaRank);
+        rank = candidate < distance ? candidateRank : min(rank, candidateRank);
+        distance = candidate;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void initializeDeviceMatrices(unsigned int* dist, unsigned int* ranks,
+                                         size_t stride, size_t numNodes) {
+    const size_t count = stride * stride;
+    for (size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < count; index += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x) {
+        const size_t row = index / stride;
+        const size_t col = index % stride;
+        if (row >= numNodes || col >= numNodes) {
+            dist[index] = row == col ? 0 : INF;
+        }
+        ranks[index] = 0;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void updatePivot(unsigned int* dist, unsigned int* ranks,
+                            size_t stride, unsigned int pivot) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int tile[TILE][TILE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int tileRank[TILE][TILE];
+    const unsigned int col = threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t base = static_cast<size_t>(pivot) * TILE;
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int r = 0; r < TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
+        const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t index = (base + row) * stride + base + col;
+        tile[row][col] = dist[index];
+        tileRank[row][col] = ranks[index];
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll 1
+    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int r = 0; r < TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
+            const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+            // With a zero diagonal these elements cannot improve. Leaving
+            // them untouched also keeps the operands read by all threads
+            // stable until the barrier, without shared-memory data races.
+            if (row != k && col != k) {
+                unsigned int value = tile[row][col];
+                unsigned int rank = tileRank[row][col];
+                relax(value, rank, tile[row][k] + tile[k][col],
+                      tileRank[row][k], tileRank[k][col], base + k + 1);
+                tile[row][col] = value;
+                tileRank[row][col] = rank;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int r = 0; r < TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
+        const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t index = (base + row) * stride + base + col;
+        dist[index] = tile[row][col];
+        ranks[index] = tileRank[row][col];
+    }
+}
+
+// Row and column tiles use the completed pivot tile. Blocks operate on disjoint
+// tiles, and each k only reads an unchanged row/column of its evolving tile.
+__global__ void updatePanels(unsigned int* dist, unsigned int* ranks,
+                             size_t stride, unsigned int pivot) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int diagonal[TILE][TILE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int diagonalRank[TILE][TILE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int panel[TILE][TILE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int panelRank[TILE][TILE];
+    const bool columnPanel = blockIdx.y != 0;
+    const unsigned int other = blockIdx.x + (blockIdx.x >= pivot);
+    const size_t pivotBase = static_cast<size_t>(pivot) * TILE;
+    const size_t rowBase = columnPanel ? static_cast<size_t>(other) * TILE : pivotBase;
+    const size_t colBase = columnPanel ? pivotBase : static_cast<size_t>(other) * TILE;
+    const unsigned int col = threadIdx.x;
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int r = 0; r < TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
+        const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t diagonalIndex = (pivotBase + row) * stride + pivotBase + col;
+        const size_t index = (rowBase + row) * stride + colBase + col;
+        diagonal[row][col] = dist[diagonalIndex];
+        diagonalRank[row][col] = ranks[diagonalIndex];
+        panel[row][col] = dist[index];
+        panelRank[row][col] = ranks[index];
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll 1
+    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int r = 0; r < TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
+            const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+            if (columnPanel ? col != k : row != k) {
+                unsigned int value = panel[row][col];
+                unsigned int rank = panelRank[row][col];
+                if (columnPanel) {
+                    relax(value, rank, panel[row][k] + diagonal[k][col],
+                          panelRank[row][k], diagonalRank[k][col], pivotBase + k + 1);
+                } else {
+                    relax(value, rank, diagonal[row][k] + panel[k][col],
+                          diagonalRank[row][k], panelRank[k][col], pivotBase + k + 1);
                 }
+                panel[row][col] = value;
+                panelRank[row][col] = rank;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int r = 0; r < TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
+        const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t index = (rowBase + row) * stride + colBase + col;
+        dist[index] = panel[row][col];
+        ranks[index] = panelRank[row][col];
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ size_t skipPivot(unsigned int coordinate,
+                                           unsigned int pivot) {
+    return coordinate + static_cast<size_t>(coordinate / TILE >= pivot) * TILE;
+}
+
+// Each block updates four 32x32 tiles using two 64x32 panels. All eight results
+// owned by a thread stay in registers throughout the reduction over k.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(TILE * THREAD_ROWS, 2)
+void updateRemaining(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                     unsigned int* __restrict__ ranks,
+                     size_t stride, unsigned int pivot) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int left[OUTPUT_TILE][TILE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int leftRank[OUTPUT_TILE][TILE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int right[TILE][OUTPUT_TILE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int rightRank[TILE][OUTPUT_TILE];
+    unsigned int values[OUTPUT_TILE / THREAD_ROWS][2];
+    unsigned int valueRanks[OUTPUT_TILE / THREAD_ROWS][2];
+    const unsigned int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const unsigned int rowBase = blockIdx.y * OUTPUT_TILE;
+    const unsigned int colBase = blockIdx.x * OUTPUT_TILE;
+    const size_t pivotBase = static_cast<size_t>(pivot) * TILE;
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int r = 0; r < OUTPUT_TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
+        const unsigned int localRow = ty + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t row = skipPivot(rowBase + localRow, pivot);
+        const size_t index = row * stride + pivotBase + tx;
+        left[localRow][tx] = row < stride ? dist[index] : INF;
+        leftRank[localRow][tx] = row < stride ? ranks[index] : 0;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int c = 0; c < 2; ++c) {
+            const size_t col = skipPivot(colBase + tx + c * TILE, pivot);
+            const size_t resultIndex = row * stride + col;
+            values[r][c] = row < stride && col < stride ? dist[resultIndex] : INF;
+            valueRanks[r][c] = row < stride && col < stride ? ranks[resultIndex] : 0;
+        }
+    }
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int r = 0; r < TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
+        const unsigned int localRow = ty + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int c = 0; c < 2; ++c) {
+            const unsigned int localCol = tx + c * TILE;
+            const size_t col = skipPivot(colBase + localCol, pivot);
+            const size_t index = (pivotBase + localRow) * stride + col;
+            right[localRow][localCol] = col < stride ? dist[index] : INF;
+            rightRank[localRow][localCol] = col < stride ? ranks[index] : 0;
+        }
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll 1
+    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {
+        const unsigned int viaRank = pivotBase + k + 1;
+        unsigned int b[2];
+        unsigned int bRank[2];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int c = 0; c < 2; ++c) {
+            b[c] = right[k][tx + c * TILE];
+            bRank[c] = max(rightRank[k][tx + c * TILE], viaRank);
+        }
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int r = 0; r < OUTPUT_TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
+            const unsigned int a = left[ty + r * THREAD_ROWS][k];
+            const unsigned int aRank = leftRank[ty + r * THREAD_ROWS][k];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned int c = 0; c < 2; ++c) {
+                relax(values[r][c], valueRanks[r][c], a + b[c], aRank, bRank[c], 0);
             }
         }
     }
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int r = 0; r < OUTPUT_TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
+        const size_t row = skipPivot(rowBase + ty + r * THREAD_ROWS, pivot);
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int c = 0; c < 2; ++c) {
+            const size_t col = skipPivot(colBase + tx + c * TILE, pivot);
+            if (row < stride && col < stride) {
+                const size_t index = row * stride + col;
+                dist[index] = values[r][c];
+                ranks[index] = valueRanks[r][c];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void restorePathMatrix(unsigned int* ranks, size_t stride) {
+    const size_t count = stride * stride;
+    for (size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < count; index += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x) {
+        const unsigned int rank = ranks[index];
+        ranks[index] = rank ? rank - 1 : static_cast<unsigned int>(index / stride);
+    }
+}
+
+} // namespace
+
+void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
+                   const size_t numNodes) {
+    if (numNodes == 0) return;
+    const size_t stride = (numNodes + TILE - 1) / TILE * TILE;
+    const unsigned int tiles = stride / TILE;
+    const size_t pitch = stride * sizeof(unsigned int);
+    const size_t hostPitch = numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int);
+    DeviceBuffer deviceDist(stride * pitch);
+    DeviceBuffer deviceRanks(stride * pitch);
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy2D(deviceDist.data, pitch, dist.data(), hostPitch,
+                           hostPitch, numNodes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "upload distances");
+
+    const unsigned int linearBlocks = static_cast<unsigned int>(
+        std::min((stride * stride + 255) / 256, static_cast<size_t>(65535)));
+    initializeDeviceMatrices<<<linearBlocks, 256>>>(deviceDist.data, deviceRanks.data,
+                                                   stride, numNodes);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "initialize device matrices");
+    const dim3 threads(TILE, THREAD_ROWS);
+    const dim3 remainingGrid(tiles / 2, tiles / 2);
+    for (unsigned int pivot = 0; pivot < tiles; ++pivot) {
+        // Launches in the default stream provide the global dependencies
+        // between phases; no host synchronization is needed inside the loop.
+        updatePivot<<<1, threads>>>(deviceDist.data, deviceRanks.data, stride, pivot);
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "update pivot tile");
+        if (tiles > 1) {
+            updatePanels<<<dim3(tiles - 1, 2), threads>>>(
+                deviceDist.data, deviceRanks.data, stride, pivot);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "update pivot row and column");
+            updateRemaining<<<remainingGrid, threads>>>(
+                deviceDist.data, deviceRanks.data, stride, pivot);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "update remaining tiles");
+        }
+    }
+    restorePathMatrix<<<linearBlocks, 256>>>(deviceRanks.data, stride);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "restore path matrix");
+    // Synchronous downloads include execution in the reported benchmark time.
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy2D(dist.data(), hostPitch, deviceDist.data, pitch,
+                           hostPitch, numNodes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "download distances");
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy2D(path.data(), hostPitch, deviceRanks.data, pitch,
+                           hostPitch, numNodes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "download paths");
 }

+namespace {
+
+constexpr unsigned int TILE = 32;
+constexpr unsigned int THREAD_ROWS = 16;
+constexpr unsigned int OUTPUT_TILE = 2 * TILE;
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error (%s): %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+struct DeviceBuffer {
+    unsigned int* data = nullptr;
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t bytes) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&data, bytes), "allocate device matrix");
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { cudaFree(data); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+};
+
+// A rank is one plus the largest internal vertex on a path; a direct edge has
+// rank zero. Among equal distances retain the smallest rank. This reproduces
+// the original ascending-k, strict-improvement path matrix even though tiling
+// changes the order in which paths are discovered.
+__device__ __forceinline__ void relax(unsigned int& distance, unsigned int& rank,
+                                     unsigned int candidate, unsigned int leftRank,
+                                     unsigned int rightRank, unsigned int viaRank) {
+    if (candidate <= distance) {
+        const unsigned int candidateRank = max(max(leftRank, rightRank), viaRank);
+        rank = candidate < distance ? candidateRank : min(rank, candidateRank);
+        distance = candidate;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void initializeDeviceMatrices(unsigned int* dist, unsigned int* ranks,
+                                         size_t stride, size_t numNodes) {
+    const size_t count = stride * stride;
+    for (size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < count; index += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x) {
+        const size_t row = index / stride;
+        const size_t col = index % stride;
+        if (row >= numNodes || col >= numNodes) {
+            dist[index] = row == col ? 0 : INF;
+        }
+        ranks[index] = 0;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void updatePivot(unsigned int* dist, unsigned int* ranks,
+                            size_t stride, unsigned int pivot) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int tile[TILE][TILE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int tileRank[TILE][TILE];
+    const unsigned int col = threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t base = static_cast<size_t>(pivot) * TILE;
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int r = 0; r < TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
+        const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t index = (base + row) * stride + base + col;
+        tile[row][col] = dist[index];
+        tileRank[row][col] = ranks[index];
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll 1
+    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int r = 0; r < TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
+            const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+            // With a zero diagonal these elements cannot improve. Leaving
+            // them untouched also keeps the operands read by all threads
+            // stable until the barrier, without shared-memory data races.
+            if (row != k && col != k) {
+                unsigned int value = tile[row][col];
+                unsigned int rank = tileRank[row][col];
+                relax(value, rank, tile[row][k] + tile[k][col],
+                      tileRank[row][k], tileRank[k][col], base + k + 1);
+                tile[row][col] = value;
+                tileRank[row][col] = rank;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int r = 0; r < TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
+        const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t index = (base + row) * stride + base + col;
+        dist[index] = tile[row][col];
+        ranks[index] = tileRank[row][col];
+    }
+}
+
+// Row and column tiles use the completed pivot tile. Blocks operate on disjoint
+// tiles, and each k only reads an unchanged row/column of its evolving tile.
+__global__ void updatePanels(unsigned int* dist, unsigned int* ranks,
+                             size_t stride, unsigned int pivot) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int diagonal[TILE][TILE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int diagonalRank[TILE][TILE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int panel[TILE][TILE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int panelRank[TILE][TILE];
+    const bool columnPanel = blockIdx.y != 0;
+    const unsigned int other = blockIdx.x + (blockIdx.x >= pivot);
+    const size_t pivotBase = static_cast<size_t>(pivot) * TILE;
+    const size_t rowBase = columnPanel ? static_cast<size_t>(other) * TILE : pivotBase;
+    const size_t colBase = columnPanel ? pivotBase : static_cast<size_t>(other) * TILE;
+    const unsigned int col = threadIdx.x;
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int r = 0; r < TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
+        const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t diagonalIndex = (pivotBase + row) * stride + pivotBase + col;
+        const size_t index = (rowBase + row) * stride + colBase + col;
+        diagonal[row][col] = dist[diagonalIndex];
+        diagonalRank[row][col] = ranks[diagonalIndex];
+        panel[row][col] = dist[index];
+        panelRank[row][col] = ranks[index];
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll 1
+    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int r = 0; r < TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
+            const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+            if (columnPanel ? col != k : row != k) {
+                unsigned int value = panel[row][col];
+                unsigned int rank = panelRank[row][col];
+                if (columnPanel) {
+                    relax(value, rank, panel[row][k] + diagonal[k][col],
+                          panelRank[row][k], diagonalRank[k][col], pivotBase + k + 1);
+                } else {
+                    relax(value, rank, diagonal[row][k] + panel[k][col],
+                          diagonalRank[row][k], panelRank[k][col], pivotBase + k + 1);
                 }
+                panel[row][col] = value;
+                panelRank[row][col] = rank;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int r = 0; r < TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
+        const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t index = (rowBase + row) * stride + colBase + col;
+        dist[index] = panel[row][col];
+        ranks[index] = panelRank[row][col];
+    }
+}

+__device__ __forceinline__ size_t skipPivot(unsigned int coordinate,
+                                           unsigned int pivot) {
+    return coordinate + static_cast<size_t>(coordinate / TILE >= pivot) * TILE;
+}
+
+// Each block updates four 32x32 tiles using two 64x32 panels. All eight results
+// owned by a thread stay in registers throughout the reduction over k.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(TILE * THREAD_ROWS, 2)
+void updateRemaining(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                     unsigned int* __restrict__ ranks,
+                     size_t stride, unsigned int pivot) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int left[OUTPUT_TILE][TILE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int leftRank[OUTPUT_TILE][TILE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int right[TILE][OUTPUT_TILE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int rightRank[TILE][OUTPUT_TILE];
+    unsigned int values[OUTPUT_TILE / THREAD_ROWS][2];
+    unsigned int valueRanks[OUTPUT_TILE / THREAD_ROWS][2];
+    const unsigned int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const unsigned int rowBase = blockIdx.y * OUTPUT_TILE;
+    const unsigned int colBase = blockIdx.x * OUTPUT_TILE;
+    const size_t pivotBase = static_cast<size_t>(pivot) * TILE;
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int r = 0; r < OUTPUT_TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
+        const unsigned int localRow = ty + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t row = skipPivot(rowBase + localRow, pivot);
+        const size_t index = row * stride + pivotBase + tx;
+        left[localRow][tx] = row < stride ? dist[index] : INF;
+        leftRank[localRow][tx] = row < stride ? ranks[index] : 0;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int c = 0; c < 2; ++c) {
+            const size_t col = skipPivot(colBase + tx + c * TILE, pivot);
+            const size_t resultIndex = row * stride + col;
+            values[r][c] = row < stride && col < stride ? dist[resultIndex] : INF;
+            valueRanks[r][c] = row < stride && col < stride ? ranks[resultIndex] : 0;
+        }
+    }
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int r = 0; r < TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
+        const unsigned int localRow = ty + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int c = 0; c < 2; ++c) {
+            const unsigned int localCol = tx + c * TILE;
+            const size_t col = skipPivot(colBase + localCol, pivot);
+            const size_t index = (pivotBase + localRow) * stride + col;
+            right[localRow][localCol] = col < stride ? dist[index] : INF;
+            rightRank[localRow][localCol] = col < stride ? ranks[index] : 0;
+        }
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll 4
+    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {
+        const unsigned int viaRank = pivotBase + k + 1;
+        unsigned int b[2];
+        unsigned int bRank[2];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int c = 0; c < 2; ++c) {
+            b[c] = right[k][tx + c * TILE];
+            bRank[c] = max(rightRank[k][tx + c * TILE], viaRank);
+        }
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int r = 0; r < OUTPUT_TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
+            const unsigned int a = left[ty + r * THREAD_ROWS][k];
+            const unsigned int aRank = leftRank[ty + r * THREAD_ROWS][k];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned int c = 0; c < 2; ++c) {
+                relax(values[r][c], valueRanks[r][c], a + b[c], aRank, bRank[c], 0);
             }
         }
     }
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int r = 0; r < OUTPUT_TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
+        const size_t row = skipPivot(rowBase + ty + r * THREAD_ROWS, pivot);
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int c = 0; c < 2; ++c) {
+            const size_t col = skipPivot(colBase + tx + c * TILE, pivot);
+            if (row < stride && col < stride) {
+                const size_t index = row * stride + col;
+                dist[index] = values[r][c];
+                ranks[index] = valueRanks[r][c];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void restorePathMatrix(unsigned int* ranks, size_t stride) {
+    const size_t count = stride * stride;
+    for (size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < count; index += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x) {
+        const unsigned int rank = ranks[index];
+        ranks[index] = rank ? rank - 1 : static_cast<unsigned int>(index / stride);
+    }
+}
+
+} // namespace
+
+void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
+                   const size_t numNodes) {
+    if (numNodes == 0) return;
+    const size_t stride = (numNodes + TILE - 1) / TILE * TILE;
+    const unsigned int tiles = stride / TILE;
+    const size_t pitch = stride * sizeof(unsigned int);
+    const size_t hostPitch = numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int);
+    DeviceBuffer deviceDist(stride * pitch);
+    DeviceBuffer deviceRanks(stride * pitch);
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy2D(deviceDist.data, pitch, dist.data(), hostPitch,
+                           hostPitch, numNodes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "upload distances");
+
+    const unsigned int linearBlocks = static_cast<unsigned int>(
+        std::min((stride * stride + 255) / 256, static_cast<size_t>(65535)));
+    initializeDeviceMatrices<<<linearBlocks, 256>>>(deviceDist.data, deviceRanks.data,
+                                                   stride, numNodes);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "initialize device matrices");
+    const dim3 threads(TILE, THREAD_ROWS);
+    const dim3 remainingGrid(tiles / 2, tiles / 2);
+    for (unsigned int pivot = 0; pivot < tiles; ++pivot) {
+        // Launches in the default stream provide the global dependencies
+        // between phases; no host synchronization is needed inside the loop.
+        updatePivot<<<1, threads>>>(deviceDist.data, deviceRanks.data, stride, pivot);
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "update pivot tile");
+        if (tiles > 1) {
+            updatePanels<<<dim3(tiles - 1, 2), threads>>>(
+                deviceDist.data, deviceRanks.data, stride, pivot);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "update pivot row and column");
+            updateRemaining<<<remainingGrid, threads>>>(
+                deviceDist.data, deviceRanks.data, stride, pivot);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "update remaining tiles");
+        }
+    }
+    restorePathMatrix<<<linearBlocks, 256>>>(deviceRanks.data, stride);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "restore path matrix");
+    // Synchronous downloads include execution in the reported benchmark time.
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy2D(dist.data(), hostPitch, deviceDist.data, pitch,
+                           hostPitch, numNodes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "download distances");
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy2D(path.data(), hostPitch, deviceRanks.data, pitch,
+                           hostPitch, numNodes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "download paths");
 }

-void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,

-                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
+namespace {
+
+constexpr unsigned int TILE = 32;
+constexpr unsigned int THREAD_ROWS = 16;
+constexpr unsigned int OUTPUT_TILE = 2 * TILE;
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error (%s): %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+struct DeviceBuffer {
+    unsigned int* data = nullptr;
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t bytes) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&data, bytes), "allocate device matrix");
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { cudaFree(data); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+};
+
+// A rank is one plus the largest internal vertex on a path; a direct edge has
+// rank zero. Among equal distances retain the smallest rank. This reproduces
+// the original ascending-k, strict-improvement path matrix even though tiling
+// changes the order in which paths are discovered.
+__device__ __forceinline__ void relax(unsigned int& distance, unsigned int& rank,
+                                     unsigned int candidate, unsigned int leftRank,
+                                     unsigned int rightRank, unsigned int viaRank) {
+    if (candidate <= distance) {
+        const unsigned int candidateRank = max(max(leftRank, rightRank), viaRank);
+        rank = candidate < distance ? candidateRank : min(rank, candidateRank);
+        distance = candidate;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void initializeDeviceMatrices(unsigned int* dist, unsigned int* ranks,
+                                         size_t stride, size_t numNodes) {
+    const size_t count = stride * stride;
+    for (size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < count; index += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x) {
+        const size_t row = index / stride;
+        const size_t col = index % stride;
+        if (row >= numNodes || col >= numNodes) {
+            dist[index] = row == col ? 0 : INF;
+        }
+        ranks[index] = 0;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void updatePivot(unsigned int* dist, unsigned int* ranks,
+                            size_t stride, unsigned int pivot) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int tile[TILE][TILE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int tileRank[TILE][TILE];
+    const unsigned int col = threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t base = static_cast<size_t>(pivot) * TILE;
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int r = 0; r < TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
+        const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t index = (base + row) * stride + base + col;
+        tile[row][col] = dist[index];
+        tileRank[row][col] = ranks[index];
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll 1
+    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int r = 0; r < TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
+            const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+            // With a zero diagonal these elements cannot improve. Leaving
+            // them untouched also keeps the operands read by all threads
+            // stable until the barrier, without shared-memory data races.
+            if (row != k && col != k) {
+                unsigned int value = tile[row][col];
+                unsigned int rank = tileRank[row][col];
+                relax(value, rank, tile[row][k] + tile[k][col],
+                      tileRank[row][k], tileRank[k][col], base + k + 1);
+                tile[row][col] = value;
+                tileRank[row][col] = rank;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int r = 0; r < TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
+        const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t index = (base + row) * stride + base + col;
+        dist[index] = tile[row][col];
+        ranks[index] = tileRank[row][col];
+    }
+}
+
+// Row and column tiles use the completed pivot tile. Blocks operate on disjoint
+// tiles, and each k only reads an unchanged row/column of its evolving tile.
+__global__ void updatePanels(unsigned int* dist, unsigned int* ranks,
+                             size_t stride, unsigned int pivot) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int diagonal[TILE][TILE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int diagonalRank[TILE][TILE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int panel[TILE][TILE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int panelRank[TILE][TILE];
+    const bool columnPanel = blockIdx.y != 0;
+    const unsigned int other = blockIdx.x + (blockIdx.x >= pivot);
+    const size_t pivotBase = static_cast<size_t>(pivot) * TILE;
+    const size_t rowBase = columnPanel ? static_cast<size_t>(other) * TILE : pivotBase;
+    const size_t colBase = columnPanel ? pivotBase : static_cast<size_t>(other) * TILE;
+    const unsigned int col = threadIdx.x;
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int r = 0; r < TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
+        const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t diagonalIndex = (pivotBase + row) * stride + pivotBase + col;
+        const size_t index = (rowBase + row) * stride + colBase + col;
+        diagonal[row][col] = dist[diagonalIndex];
+        diagonalRank[row][col] = ranks[diagonalIndex];
+        panel[row][col] = dist[index];
+        panelRank[row][col] = ranks[index];
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll 1
+    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int r = 0; r < TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
+            const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+            if (columnPanel ? col != k : row != k) {
+                unsigned int value = panel[row][col];
+                unsigned int rank = panelRank[row][col];
+                if (columnPanel) {
+                    relax(value, rank, panel[row][k] + diagonal[k][col],
+                          panelRank[row][k], diagonalRank[k][col], pivotBase + k + 1);
+                } else {
+                    relax(value, rank, diagonal[row][k] + panel[k][col],
+                          diagonalRank[row][k], panelRank[k][col], pivotBase + k + 1);
                 }
+                panel[row][col] = value;
+                panelRank[row][col] = rank;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int r = 0; r < TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
+        const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t index = (rowBase + row) * stride + colBase + col;
+        dist[index] = panel[row][col];
+        ranks[index] = panelRank[row][col];
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ size_t skipPivot(unsigned int coordinate,
+                                           unsigned int pivot) {
+    return coordinate + static_cast<size_t>(coordinate / TILE >= pivot) * TILE;
+}
+
+// Each block updates four 32x32 tiles using two 64x32 panels. All eight results
+// owned by a thread stay in registers throughout the reduction over k.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(TILE * THREAD_ROWS, 2)
+void updateRemaining(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                     unsigned int* __restrict__ ranks,
+                     size_t stride, unsigned int pivot) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int left[OUTPUT_TILE][TILE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int leftRank[OUTPUT_TILE][TILE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int right[TILE][OUTPUT_TILE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int rightRank[TILE][OUTPUT_TILE];
+    unsigned int values[OUTPUT_TILE / THREAD_ROWS][2];
+    unsigned int valueRanks[OUTPUT_TILE / THREAD_ROWS][2];
+    const unsigned int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const unsigned int rowBase = blockIdx.y * OUTPUT_TILE;
+    const unsigned int colBase = blockIdx.x * OUTPUT_TILE;
+    const size_t pivotBase = static_cast<size_t>(pivot) * TILE;
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int r = 0; r < OUTPUT_TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
+        const unsigned int localRow = ty + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t row = skipPivot(rowBase + localRow, pivot);
+        const size_t index = row * stride + pivotBase + tx;
+        left[localRow][tx] = row < stride ? dist[index] : INF;
+        leftRank[localRow][tx] = row < stride ? ranks[index] : 0;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int c = 0; c < 2; ++c) {
+            const size_t col = skipPivot(colBase + tx + c * TILE, pivot);
+            const size_t resultIndex = row * stride + col;
+            values[r][c] = row < stride && col < stride ? dist[resultIndex] : INF;
+            valueRanks[r][c] = row < stride && col < stride ? ranks[resultIndex] : 0;
+        }
+    }
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int r = 0; r < TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
+        const unsigned int localRow = ty + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int c = 0; c < 2; ++c) {
+            const unsigned int localCol = tx + c * TILE;
+            const size_t col = skipPivot(colBase + localCol, pivot);
+            const size_t index = (pivotBase + localRow) * stride + col;
+            right[localRow][localCol] = col < stride ? dist[index] : INF;
+            rightRank[localRow][localCol] = col < stride ? ranks[index] : 0;
+        }
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll 4
+    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {
+        const unsigned int viaRank = pivotBase + k + 1;
+        unsigned int b[2];
+        unsigned int bRank[2];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int c = 0; c < 2; ++c) {
+            b[c] = right[k][tx + c * TILE];
+            bRank[c] = max(rightRank[k][tx + c * TILE], viaRank);
+        }
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int r = 0; r < OUTPUT_TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
+            const unsigned int a = left[ty + r * THREAD_ROWS][k];
+            const unsigned int aRank = leftRank[ty + r * THREAD_ROWS][k];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned int c = 0; c < 2; ++c) {
+                relax(values[r][c], valueRanks[r][c], a + b[c], aRank, bRank[c], 0);
             }
         }
     }
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int r = 0; r < OUTPUT_TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
+        const size_t row = skipPivot(rowBase + ty + r * THREAD_ROWS, pivot);
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int c = 0; c < 2; ++c) {
+            const size_t col = skipPivot(colBase + tx + c * TILE, pivot);
+            if (row < stride && col < stride) {
+                const size_t index = row * stride + col;
+                dist[index] = values[r][c];
+                ranks[index] = valueRanks[r][c];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void restorePathMatrix(unsigned int* ranks, size_t stride) {
+    const size_t count = stride * stride;
+    for (size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < count; index += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x) {
+        const unsigned int rank = ranks[index];
+        ranks[index] = rank ? rank - 1 : static_cast<unsigned int>(index / stride);
+    }
+}
+
+} // namespace
+
+void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
+                   const size_t numNodes) {
+    if (numNodes == 0) return;
+    const size_t stride = (numNodes + TILE - 1) / TILE * TILE;
+    const unsigned int tiles = stride / TILE;
+    const size_t pitch = stride * sizeof(unsigned int);
+    const size_t hostPitch = numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int);
+    DeviceBuffer deviceDist(stride * pitch);
+    DeviceBuffer deviceRanks(stride * pitch);
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy2D(deviceDist.data, pitch, dist.data(), hostPitch,
+                           hostPitch, numNodes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "upload distances");
+
+    const unsigned int linearBlocks = static_cast<unsigned int>(
+        std::min((stride * stride + 255) / 256, static_cast<size_t>(65535)));
+    initializeDeviceMatrices<<<linearBlocks, 256>>>(deviceDist.data, deviceRanks.data,
+                                                   stride, numNodes);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "initialize device matrices");
+    const dim3 threads(TILE, THREAD_ROWS);
+    const dim3 remainingGrid(tiles / 2, tiles / 2);
+    for (unsigned int pivot = 0; pivot < tiles; ++pivot) {
+        // Launches in the default stream provide the global dependencies
+        // between phases; no host synchronization is needed inside the loop.
+        updatePivot<<<1, threads>>>(deviceDist.data, deviceRanks.data, stride, pivot);
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "update pivot tile");
+        if (tiles > 1) {
+            updatePanels<<<dim3(tiles - 1, 2), threads>>>(
+                deviceDist.data, deviceRanks.data, stride, pivot);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "update pivot row and column");
+            updateRemaining<<<remainingGrid, threads>>>(
+                deviceDist.data, deviceRanks.data, stride, pivot);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "update remaining tiles");
+        }
+    }
+    restorePathMatrix<<<linearBlocks, 256>>>(deviceRanks.data, stride);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "restore path matrix");
+    // Synchronous downloads include execution in the reported benchmark time.
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy2D(dist.data(), hostPitch, deviceDist.data, pitch,
+                           hostPitch, numNodes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "download distances");
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy2D(path.data(), hostPitch, deviceRanks.data, pitch,

-void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,

+namespace {
+
+constexpr unsigned int TILE = 32;
+constexpr unsigned int THREAD_ROWS = 16;
+constexpr unsigned int OUTPUT_TILE = 2 * TILE;
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error (%s): %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+struct DeviceBuffer {
+    unsigned int* data = nullptr;
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t bytes) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&data, bytes), "allocate device matrix");
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { cudaFree(data); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+};
+
+// A rank is one plus the largest internal vertex on a path; a direct edge has
+// rank zero. Among equal distances retain the smallest rank. This reproduces
+// the original ascending-k, strict-improvement path matrix even though tiling
+// changes the order in which paths are discovered.
+__device__ __forceinline__ void relax(unsigned int& distance, unsigned int& rank,
+                                     unsigned int candidate, unsigned int leftRank,
+                                     unsigned int rightRank, unsigned int viaRank) {
+    if (candidate <= distance) {
+        const unsigned int candidateRank = max(max(leftRank, rightRank), viaRank);
+        rank = candidate < distance ? candidateRank : min(rank, candidateRank);
+        distance = candidate;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void initializeDeviceMatrices(unsigned int* dist, unsigned int* ranks,
+                                         size_t stride, size_t numNodes) {
+    const size_t count = stride * stride;
+    for (size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < count; index += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x) {
+        const size_t row = index / stride;
+        const size_t col = index % stride;
+        if (row >= numNodes || col >= numNodes) {
+            dist[index] = row == col ? 0 : INF;
+        }
+        ranks[index] = 0;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void updatePivot(unsigned int* dist, unsigned int* ranks,
+                            size_t stride, unsigned int pivot) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int tile[TILE][TILE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int tileRank[TILE][TILE];
+    const unsigned int col = threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t base = static_cast<size_t>(pivot) * TILE;
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int r = 0; r < TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
+        const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t index = (base + row) * stride + base + col;
+        tile[row][col] = dist[index];
+        tileRank[row][col] = ranks[index];
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll 1
+    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int r = 0; r < TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
+            const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+            // With a zero diagonal these elements cannot improve. Leaving
+            // them untouched also keeps the operands read by all threads
+            // stable until the barrier, without shared-memory data races.
+            if (row != k && col != k) {
+                unsigned int value = tile[row][col];
+                unsigned int rank = tileRank[row][col];
+                relax(value, rank, tile[row][k] + tile[k][col],
+                      tileRank[row][k], tileRank[k][col], base + k + 1);
+                tile[row][col] = value;
+                tileRank[row][col] = rank;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int r = 0; r < TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
+        const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t index = (base + row) * stride + base + col;
+        dist[index] = tile[row][col];
+        ranks[index] = tileRank[row][col];
+    }
+}
+
+// Row and column tiles use the completed pivot tile. Blocks operate on disjoint
+// tiles, and each k only reads an unchanged row/column of its evolving tile.
+__global__ void updatePanels(unsigned int* dist, unsigned int* ranks,
+                             size_t stride, unsigned int pivot) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int diagonal[TILE][TILE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int diagonalRank[TILE][TILE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int panel[TILE][TILE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int panelRank[TILE][TILE];
+    const bool columnPanel = blockIdx.y != 0;
+    const unsigned int other = blockIdx.x + (blockIdx.x >= pivot);
+    const size_t pivotBase = static_cast<size_t>(pivot) * TILE;
+    const size_t rowBase = columnPanel ? static_cast<size_t>(other) * TILE : pivotBase;
+    const size_t colBase = columnPanel ? pivotBase : static_cast<size_t>(other) * TILE;
+    const unsigned int col = threadIdx.x;
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int r = 0; r < TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
+        const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t diagonalIndex = (pivotBase + row) * stride + pivotBase + col;
+        const size_t index = (rowBase + row) * stride + colBase + col;
+        diagonal[row][col] = dist[diagonalIndex];
+        diagonalRank[row][col] = ranks[diagonalIndex];
+        panel[row][col] = dist[index];
+        panelRank[row][col] = ranks[index];
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll 1
+    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int r = 0; r < TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
+            const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+            if (columnPanel ? col != k : row != k) {
+                unsigned int value = panel[row][col];
+                unsigned int rank = panelRank[row][col];
+                if (columnPanel) {
+                    relax(value, rank, panel[row][k] + diagonal[k][col],
+                          panelRank[row][k], diagonalRank[k][col], pivotBase + k + 1);
+                } else {
+                    relax(value, rank, diagonal[row][k] + panel[k][col],
+                          diagonalRank[row][k], panelRank[k][col], pivotBase + k + 1);
                 }
+                panel[row][col] = value;
+                panelRank[row][col] = rank;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int r = 0; r < TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
+        const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t index = (rowBase + row) * stride + colBase + col;
+        dist[index] = panel[row][col];
+        ranks[index] = panelRank[row][col];
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ size_t skipPivot(unsigned int coordinate,
+                                           unsigned int pivot) {
+    return coordinate + static_cast<size_t>(coordinate / TILE >= pivot) * TILE;
+}
+
+// Each block updates four 32x32 tiles using two 64x32 panels. All eight results
+// owned by a thread stay in registers throughout the reduction over k.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(TILE * THREAD_ROWS, 2)
+void updateRemaining(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                     unsigned int* __restrict__ ranks,
+                     size_t stride, unsigned int pivot) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int left[OUTPUT_TILE][TILE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int leftRank[OUTPUT_TILE][TILE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int right[TILE][OUTPUT_TILE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int rightRank[TILE][OUTPUT_TILE];
+    unsigned int values[OUTPUT_TILE / THREAD_ROWS][2];
+    unsigned int valueRanks[OUTPUT_TILE / THREAD_ROWS][2];
+    const unsigned int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const unsigned int rowBase = blockIdx.y * OUTPUT_TILE;
+    const unsigned int colBase = blockIdx.x * OUTPUT_TILE;
+    const size_t pivotBase = static_cast<size_t>(pivot) * TILE;
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int r = 0; r < OUTPUT_TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
+        const unsigned int localRow = ty + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t row = skipPivot(rowBase + localRow, pivot);
+        const size_t index = row * stride + pivotBase + tx;
+        left[localRow][tx] = row < stride ? dist[index] : INF;
+        leftRank[localRow][tx] = row < stride ? ranks[index] : 0;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int c = 0; c < 2; ++c) {
+            const size_t col = skipPivot(colBase + tx + c * TILE, pivot);
+            const size_t resultIndex = row * stride + col;
+            values[r][c] = row < stride && col < stride ? dist[resultIndex] : INF;
+            valueRanks[r][c] = row < stride && col < stride ? ranks[resultIndex] : 0;
+        }
+    }
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int r = 0; r < TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
+        const unsigned int localRow = ty + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int c = 0; c < 2; ++c) {
+            const unsigned int localCol = tx + c * TILE;
+            const size_t col = skipPivot(colBase + localCol, pivot);
+            const size_t index = (pivotBase + localRow) * stride + col;
+            right[localRow][localCol] = col < stride ? dist[index] : INF;
+            rightRank[localRow][localCol] = col < stride ? ranks[index] : 0;
+        }
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll 4
+    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {
+        const unsigned int viaRank = pivotBase + k + 1;
+        unsigned int b[2];
+        unsigned int bRank[2];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int c = 0; c < 2; ++c) {
+            b[c] = right[k][tx + c * TILE];
+            bRank[c] = max(rightRank[k][tx + c * TILE], viaRank);
+        }
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int r = 0; r < OUTPUT_TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
+            const unsigned int a = left[ty + r * THREAD_ROWS][k];
+            const unsigned int aRank = leftRank[ty + r * THREAD_ROWS][k];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned int c = 0; c < 2; ++c) {
+                relax(values[r][c], valueRanks[r][c], a + b[c], aRank, bRank[c], 0);
             }
         }
     }
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int r = 0; r < OUTPUT_TILE / THREAD_ROWS; ++r) {
+        const size_t row = skipPivot(rowBase + ty + r * THREAD_ROWS, pivot);
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int c = 0; c < 2; ++c) {
+            const size_t col = skipPivot(colBase + tx + c * TILE, pivot);
+            if (row < stride && col < stride) {
+                const size_t index = row * stride + col;
+                dist[index] = values[r][c];
+                ranks[index] = valueRanks[r][c];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void restorePathMatrix(unsigned int* ranks, size_t stride) {
+    const size_t count = stride * stride;
+    for (size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < count; index += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x) {
+        const unsigned int rank = ranks[index];
+        ranks[index] = rank ? rank - 1 : static_cast<unsigned int>(index / stride);
+    }
+}
+
+} // namespace
+
+void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
+                   const size_t numNodes) {
+    if (numNodes == 0) return;
+    const size_t stride = (numNodes + TILE - 1) / TILE * TILE;
+    const unsigned int tiles = stride / TILE;
+    const size_t pitch = stride * sizeof(unsigned int);
+    const size_t hostPitch = numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int);
+    DeviceBuffer deviceDist(stride * pitch);
+    DeviceBuffer deviceRanks(stride * pitch);
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy2D(deviceDist.data, pitch, dist.data(), hostPitch,
+                           hostPitch, numNodes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "upload distances");
+
+    const unsigned int linearBlocks = static_cast<unsigned int>(
+        std::min((stride * stride + 255) / 256, static_cast<size_t>(65535)));
+    initializeDeviceMatrices<<<linearBlocks, 256>>>(deviceDist.data, deviceRanks.data,
+                                                   stride, numNodes);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "initialize device matrices");
+    const dim3 threads(TILE, THREAD_ROWS);
+    const dim3 remainingGrid(tiles / 2, tiles / 2);
+    for (unsigned int pivot = 0; pivot < tiles; ++pivot) {
+        // Launches in the default stream provide the global dependencies
+        // between phases; no host synchronization is needed inside the loop.
+        updatePivot<<<1, threads>>>(deviceDist.data, deviceRanks.data, stride, pivot);
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "update pivot tile");
+        if (tiles > 1) {
+            updatePanels<<<dim3(tiles - 1, 2), threads>>>(
+                deviceDist.data, deviceRanks.data, stride, pivot);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "update pivot row and column");
+            updateRemaining<<<remainingGrid, threads>>>(
+                deviceDist.data, deviceRanks.data, stride, pivot);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "update remaining tiles");
+        }
+    }
+    restorePathMatrix<<<linearBlocks, 256>>>(deviceRanks.data, stride);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "restore path matrix");
+    // Synchronous downloads include execution in the reported benchmark time.
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy2D(dist.data(), hostPitch, deviceDist.data, pitch,
+                           hostPitch, numNodes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "download distances");
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy2D(path.data(), hostPitch, deviceRanks.data, pitch,
+                           hostPitch, numNodes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "download paths");
 }
